- **Enums:** 165.
- **Names:** the longest 165 characters, 1,058 over 63.
- **Presets withheld:** 310 constructors of 298 types, 346 of the 356 preset constructions with a lookup by name.
- **Engines:** 161 engines, 202 input sets; 37 engine parameters, 925 pairs formed, 8 refused at the call, 264 calculations forming no pair.
- **Not crossing:** 2,467 members and types.

## Presets by name

Each coded preset's constructor is withheld; the convention set's lookup gives the same preset by its name. The recorder
writes a constructor call whose arguments match a preset's as that lookup.

## Matrix and Vector arguments their members write

Found from IL: the member, an override or an implementation changes the argument, or passes it to a member that does.
Each crosses in and out (`vector&`, `matrix&`): `double*` with its counts, written back after the call.

- `nql_Constraint_Update`: `p`
- `nql_CostFunction_Gradient`: `grad`
- `nql_CostFunction_Jacobian`: `jac`
- `nql_CostFunction_ValueAndGradient`: `grad`

- `nql_LeastSquareFunction_Gradient`: `grad`
- `nql_LeastSquareFunction_ValueAndGradient`: `grad`

- `nql_LeastSquareProblem_TargetAndValue`: `target`
- `nql_LeastSquareProblem_TargetValueAndGradient`: `fct2fit`
- `nql_LeastSquareProblem_TargetValueAndGradient`: `gradFct2fit`
- `nql_LeastSquareProblem_TargetValueAndGradient`: `target`
